```js
## 为什么要有这个目录

比赛硬要求「10 分钟极速复现」。评委的机器上不会有 API Key，也不该为了看一个
demo 去申请一个。所以 CrossExam 默认跑在 replay 模式：所有模型调用改从本目录
读取当初的响应，一次网络请求都不发。

指纹只由提示词内容决定，**不含模型名**——换模型不会让整盘卡带失效。

## 规则

- 文件名 = digestFor(request) = 提示词内容的 sha256 前 24 位
- 记录里含当时的 usage、purpose、model 与流式分片，供事后追溯
- 缺失记录时回放**报错**而不是静默降级（见 packages/server/src/llm/replay.ts）
- 用 CROSSEXAM_MODE=record 联网跑一次即可补录

或真人的输出**。判别方式很简单：卡带里的 `model` 字段固定为 `scripted-demo`。
```

```ts
/**
 * 离线演示的两道门禁。
 *
 * 为什么用真卡带跑一遍 HTTP：这是「评委点一下就能看到完整质询」这句话的机器证明。
 * 卡带与提示词一旦脱节（改了一个字、换了一版渲染），这条测试就红——
 * 它比任何人肉回归都可靠，因为提示词指纹是算出来的，不是记出来的。
 *
 * 第二条测试盯的是**静默失败**：编排层对单个阶段的失败是逐项容错的，
 * 演示脚本抛错时合成跑照样"成功"，只有回放到那一步才炸。
 * 所以生成器必须自己点名"五个阶段是否都录到了"。
 */

import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { seedDemoCassettes } from '../src/demo-seed.ts';
import { createApp } from '../src/http/app.ts';
import type { RunSummary } from '../src/http/app.ts';
import { listCassettes, readCassette } from '../src/llm/cassette.ts';
import { createReplayClient } from '../src/llm/replay.ts';
import { closeDatabase, openDatabase } from '../src/store/index.ts';

/** 仓库里已提交的卡带目录（相对本测试文件：packages/server/test -> 仓库根） */
const FIXTURE_DIR = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../fixtures/llm', import.meta.url));
const ALL_PURPOSES = ['extract', 'probe', 'answer', 'verdict', 'suggest'];

const tempDirs: string[] = [];

function newTempDir(tag: string): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'crossexam-' + tag + '-'));
  tempDirs.push(dir);
  return dir;
}

afterEach(() => {
  while (tempDirs.length > 0) {
    const dir = tempDirs.pop();
    if (dir !== undefined) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});

/** 走真实 HTTP 接口跑一遍示例简历：创建会话 -> 同步开跑 -> 读摘要 */
async function runDemoOverHttp(cassetteDir: string): Promise<RunSummary> {
  const db = openDatabase({ path: ':memory:' });
  try {
    const app = createApp({ db, client: createReplayClient(cassetteDir), cassetteDir });
    const created = await app.request('/api/sessions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ useDemo: true }),
    });
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    const session = (await created.json()) as { id: string };

    const res = await app.request('/api/sessions/' + session.id + '/run?stream=0', { method: 'POST' });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    return (await res.json()) as RunSummary;
  } finally {
    closeDatabase(db);
  }
}

describe('离线演示（合成卡带）', () => {
  it('仓库里的卡带能让示例简历在零网络下跑完整场质询', async () => {
    const summary = await runDemoOverHttp(FIXTURE_DIR);

    expect(summary.claims).toBe(5);
    expect(summary.rejectedClaims).toBe(1);
    expect(summary.rounds).toBe(3);
    expect(summary.probes).toBe(10);
    expect(summary.suggestions).toBe(4);
    // 四种判定各有戏：站得住 / 表述过度 / 无法回答 / 证据缺失
    const kinds = summary.verdicts.map((verdict) => verdict.kind).sort();
    expect(kinds).toEqual(['overstated', 'solid', 'unanswerable', 'unanswerable', 'unsupported']);
    // 第三轮没有新问题就收尾，而不是空转
    expect(summary.notices.some((notice) => notice.indexOf('没有任何新的追问') >= 0)).toBe(true);
    expect(summary.usage.outputTokens).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('生成器写出的卡带覆盖五个阶段，且两遍结果一致', async () => {
    const dir = newTempDir('demo-seed');
    const report = await seedDemoCassettes({ dir });

    expect(report.problems).toEqual([]);
    expect(report.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(report.replay).toEqual(report.record);

    const summaries = listCassettes(dir);
    expect(summaries).toHaveLength(report.entries.length);
    for (const purpose of ALL_PURPOSES) {
      expect(summaries.filter((item) => item.purpose === purpose).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
    // 合成数据必须一眼可辨：模型名固定，录制时间固定（重跑逐字节相同）
    expect(summaries.every((item) => item.model === 'scripted-demo')).toBe(true);
    const first = summaries[0];
    expect(first).toBeDefined();
    const entry = first === undefined ? null : readCassette(dir, first.digest);
    expect(entry?.recordedAt).toBe('2026-10-26T00:00:00.000Z');
    expect(entry?.version).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

## Session 4（2026-10-26）P2 收尾：离线演示卡带

### 这一段做了什么

把「没有 API Key 也能 10 分钟复现」从一句承诺变成一条命令：`npm run seed:demo`
铺好 29 盘卡带，之后 `CROSSEXAM_MODE=replay`（默认值）下点一次示例简历就能跑完
一整场质询——抽取、三位面试官的并发追问、逐条判定、反推建议，全程零网络。

四件事：

1. **确定性演示剧本**（scripted-demo.ts）：写死谁问什么、候选人怎么答、
   每条主张怎么判、建议怎么改；它按**真实提示词**渲染回答，所以录下来的卡带
   与将来回放时的提示词逐字节对应。
2. **边跑边录的客户端**：按 `digestFor(request)` 写盘，录完立刻用只读回放客户端
   在另一个内存库里重跑一遍，**两遍结果签名必须完全一致**（否则退出码 1）。
3. **seed:demo 命令**（bin/seed-demo.ts）：打印卡带目录、各阶段条数、两遍摘要、
   自检结论；自检不过就失败退出，避免残缺卡带被提交。
4. **漂移门禁**（demo-cassette.test.ts）：用仓库里已提交的卡带走真实 HTTP 跑一遍
   完整沙盘。提示词改一个字，指纹就变，这条测试立刻红——它比人肉回归可靠，
   因为指纹是算出来的。

### 剧本刻意覆盖的四种判定与两类代码级收敛

| 主张 | 剧本走向 | 想证明什么 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C1 首屏 2.4s→0.8s | 答得出，且给出指标口径 | 站得住：模型想放过时，代码不额外加戏 |
| C2 QPS 800→5000 | 承认前后口径不一致 | 表述过度：判定要落到具体口径差异上 |
| C3 RBAC 覆盖 12 个业务线 | 连续两次答不上来 | 代码强制「无法回答」，不靠模型自觉 |
| C4 熟悉 TypeScript / Node.js | 同上 | 同上，验证收敛与主张类型无关 |
| C5 年度技术突破奖 | 模型想判站得住，却给不出证据引文 | 证据缺失：缺证据自动降级，模型说了不算 |

另有一条**故意锚不到**的主张（引文「精通分布式事务与高并发架构」不在简历里），
用来演示锚定机制会把幻觉式主张直接拦在门外，而不是把它摆上台去问。

### 这一段抓到的两个真 Bug

1. **标题行取值取到空字符串，整段回答阶段静默失败**（见 F12）。
   合成的第一版「成功」退出，磁盘上却只有 16/27 盘卡带——10 条回答与 1 条建议
   全缺，stderr 干干净净。直到回放才报「找不到这一条记录」。
   根因不在录制器，而在**编排层的逐项容错**：单个阶段失败被吞掉，整体照样算跑完。
   修法两条：修 `lineAfter` 的取行逻辑；生成器自己点名五个阶段是否都录到。
2. **insertSession 的 llmMode 是 `StoredLlmMode` 不是 `string`**：`tsc --noEmit`
   直接报 TS2322。这条没什么可讲的，但它证明了门禁里 typecheck 不是摆设——
   同一个提交如果只跑 vitest，类型错误会一路进仓库。

### 门禁

`npm run verify` **exit 0**：typecheck + **22 个测试文件 / 203 条断言** + 冒烟 9 项 + build。
`npm run seed:demo` 自检通过：3 轮 · 5 条主张 · 10 次追问 · 判定 站得住 1 / 表述过度 1 /
无法回答 2 / 证据缺失 1 · 4 条改写建议 · 拦下 1 条 · 估算 token 23533。

### 提交

本轮 4 个细粒度提交（feat 3 / test 1），工作区干净。

### 下一步

P3 前端：Vite + React + Tailwind，质询舞台做流式可视化与思考过程展示，
判定视图与报告下载。这批卡带同时是 P4 演示视频的素材。

F12 content:

```
## F12. 录制端的「成功」不等于素材齐全：逐项容错会吞掉整段失败（2026-10-26）

合成演示卡带第一版跑完退出码 0，磁盘上却只有 16/27 盘卡带：10 条 answer 与
1 条 suggest 全部缺失，stderr 干干净净。直到换回放模式才炸：

    LlmError: 回放模式找不到这一条记录。用途「answer」的指纹是 11b561bbda2d225fcad8ff25，
    请用 CROSSEXAM_MODE=record 联网跑一次把它录下来。

根因有两层，第二层才是真正危险的：

1. `lineAfter(prompt, '## 面试官的问题')` 取「标题之后到第一个换行」，但真实提示词里
   标题独占一行，问题在下一行，于是它返回空字符串。10 条回答全部抛
   「演示脚本没有覆盖这个问题：」。
2. **编排层对单个面试官/单条主张的失败是逐项容错的**（这是对的：一个面试官掉线不该
   拖垮整场质询），于是这 10 次抛错被当作「这一项失败，跳过」处理，整体照样算跑完。

于是失败从「响亮报错」退化成「静默缺料」，而缺料的证据只在回放时才出现——
如果没写回放自检，这批残缺卡带会被提交进仓库，评委点开演示时才发现。

**处置**：lineAfter 先看标题行内有没有内容（有就直接返回），没有才取下一行；
生成器（demo-seed.ts）加一条「五个阶段逐个点名」的断言，缺任何一阶段即 problems 非空、
退出码 1。门禁层面由 demo-cassette.test.ts 用已提交卡带跑真实 HTTP 兜底。

**教训**：逐项容错是产品正确性，但**录制/生成类工具必须自己统计覆盖率**，
不能把「整体没抛错」当作「素材齐全」。凡是「部分失败被允许」的流程，
都要额外问一句：这次到底成功了几个？
```

Careful: this has a fenced code block with backticks in my draft. Since I'm using String.raw, no backticks allowed. Use 4-space indented block for the error text ✓ (I did). And inline backticks → use 「」.

```
## F13. String.raw 里出现反引号会当场截断程序体（2026-10-26）
```

```js
const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
const git = async (cmd: string, description: string) => {
  const r = await tools.ops_bash({ command: cmd, description, workdir: "D:\\CZB", timeoutMs: 180000 });
  const out = r.kind === "foreground" ? "exit=" + r.exitCode + NL + r.stdout.text + r.stderr.text : JSON.stringify(r);
  console.log("$ " + cmd.slice(0, 70) + NL + out + NL + "---");
```

OK. Now let me design the UI carefully — this is the visible product. Key UX (15% weight): 流式 + 思考可视化.

Layout: single-page app, three zones:
1. 左栏（投料台）: 简历文本域 + JD 文本域 + 「一键示例」按钮 + 「开始质询」按钮 + 历史会话列表。
2. 中栏（质询舞台）: 阶段时间线（extract → probe r1 → answer r1 → ... → verdict → suggest），流式卡片：主张卡片（带原文引文锚点，点击高亮原文）、面试官气泡（人设+问题+理由）、候选人回答（含引用锚点/答不上来）、判定徽章（含「被代码收敛」提示）、修改建议（before→after）。
3. 右栏（材料）: 简历原文，锚点高亮联动；顶部状态条显示 mode（离线回放/联网）、模型、用量、事件数。

Interactions:
- 点击主张/追问的引文 → 右栏简历对应区间高亮滚动。
- SSE 事件驱动：把事件归约成 UI 状态（reducer）。
- 跑完后「下载报告」按钮（/report.md）+ 「重跑」提示（会话只能跑一次 → 新建会话）。

Implementation files (keep count manageable but modular):
- src/main.tsx
- src/App.tsx (layout + state machine + SSE wiring)
- src/api.ts (types + fetch helpers + streamRun)
- src/labels.ts (中文标签)
- src/state.ts (event → view reducer: pure function, testable!)
- src/components/Intake.tsx
- src/components/StageTimeline.tsx
- src/components/ClaimCard.tsx (含追问链 + 判定 + 建议)
- src/components/ResumePane.tsx
- src/components/StatusBar.tsx
- src/index.css

The reducer (state.ts) is pure and testable — good for the "engineering quality" dimension and gives real tests: feed the 29-cassette run's events? Simpler: unit test the reducer with synthetic event sequence.

That's a big chunk of code. Let's be pragmatic: build it in 3-4 programs.

Program 1: package.json (no deps) + index.html + vite.config.ts + tsconfig.json + src/index.css + src/main.tsx + src/labels.ts. Then npm install deps.

```ts
export interface ProbeView { probe: Probe; answer?: AnswerOutcome; error?: string }

  phase: 'idle'|'running'|'done'|'failed';
  stages: StageMark[];
  activeStage: string|null;
  round: number;
  claims: ClaimView[];
  rejectedClaims: RejectedClaim[];
  rejectedProbes: RejectedProbe[];
  answers: AnswerOutcome[];
  errors: {message:string; fatal:boolean}[];
  notices: string[];

  usage: Usage;
  eventCount: number;
  summary: RunSummary | null;
  highlight: Anchor | null;  // 当前高亮锚点
}
export const initialState: SandboxState

```

SSE parsing function `parseSseChunk`? Extract a pure function `readSseStream(stream: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>, onEvent)` — testable in node? ReadableStream is global in Node 18+ ✓. Could test it. Keep it exported for the test.

Implementation of readSseStream:
```ts
export async function readSseStream(body: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>, onEvent: (e: SseEvent) => void): Promise<void> {
  const reader = body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let buffer = '';
  for (;;) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
    let idx = buffer.indexOf('\n\n');
    while (idx >= 0) {
      const raw = buffer.slice(0, idx);
      buffer = buffer.slice(idx + 2);
      const ev = parseSseBlock(raw);
      if (ev !== null) onEvent(ev);
      idx = buffer.indexOf('\n\n');
    }
  }
  buffer += decoder.decode();
  const last = parseSseBlock(buffer);
  if (last !== null) onEvent(last);
}
```
Handle \r\n: normalize raw with replace(/\r\n/g, '\n') → inside String.raw I can't use regex with... yes I can, regex is fine (no backticks). But `\r\n` inside String.raw becomes literal backslash-r-backslash-n in the output file, which is what we want in the .ts source ✓ (the file will contain the escape sequence text, which TS interprets as CR LF) ✓. Good: String.raw preserves the backslash for the file content.

parseSseBlock:
```ts
export function parseSseBlock(raw: string): SseEvent | null {
  const lines = raw.split('\n');
  let event = 'message';
  let id: string | null = null;
  const dataLines: string[] = [];
  for (const line of lines) {
    if (line.length === 0 || line.startsWith(':')) continue;
    const colon = line.indexOf(':');
    const field = colon === -1 ? line : line.slice(0, colon);
    let value = colon === -1 ? '' : line.slice(colon + 1);
    if (value.startsWith(' ')) value = value.slice(1);
    if (field === 'event') event = value;
    else if (field === 'data') dataLines.push(value);
    else if (field === 'id') id = value;
  }
  if (dataLines.length === 0) return null;
  const joined = dataLines.join('\n');
  let data: unknown = joined;
  try { data = JSON.parse(joined); } catch { data = joined; }
  return { id, event, data };
}
```
Hmm: careful with `catch {}` — optional catch binding is erasable ✓.

streamRun:
```ts
export async function streamRun(sessionId: string, opts: RunOptions, onEvent): Promise<void> {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams();
  if (opts.panel && opts.panel.length > 0) qs.set('panel', opts.panel.join(','));
  if (opts.maxClaims) qs.set('maxClaims', String(opts.maxClaims));
  if (opts.maxRounds) qs.set('maxRounds', String(opts.maxRounds));
  const suffix = qs.toString().length > 0 ? '?' + qs.toString() : '';
  const res = await fetch('/api/sessions/' + encodeURIComponent(sessionId) + '/run' + suffix, { method: 'POST', signal: opts.signal });
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(await readError(res), res.status);
  if (res.body === null) throw new ApiError('服务端没有返回流', 0);
  await readSseStream(res.body, onEvent);
}
```
Note: server route supports panel/maxClaims — does it support maxRounds? Earlier: `POST run?stream=0&panel=tech,hr&maxClaims=N`. maxRounds maybe not. Don't send maxRounds.

```ts
export interface ProbeView {
  probe: Probe;
  answer: AnswerOutcome | null;
  answerError: string | null;
  round: number;
}

export interface ClaimView {
  claim: Claim;
  probes: ProbeView[];
  verdict: VerdictOutcome | null;
  suggestion: SuggestOutcome | null;
  /** 预算关停的原因（来自 budget 事件的 closed 列表 + 追问账本） */
  closed: boolean;
}

export interface StageMark { stage: string; round: number | null; seq: number }

export type Phase = 'idle' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed';

export interface SandboxState {
  phase: Phase;
  /** 会话 id，跑的时候才有 */
  sessionId: string | null;
  stages: StageMark[];
  activeStage: string | null;
  activeRound: number | null;
  claims: ClaimView[];
  rejectedClaims: RejectedClaim[];
  rejectedProbes: RejectedProbe[];
  errors: { message: string; fatal: boolean }[];
  notices: string[];
  budget: { totalProbes: number; open: string[]; closed: string[] } | null;
  summary: RunSummary | null;
  usage: Usage;
  eventCount: number;
  /** 面板：本次跑用了哪几位面试官（从追问事件里学到） */
  interviewers: string[];
  /** 界面上当前高亮的锚点（点引文时设置） */
  focus: Anchor | null;
}
```
Actions:
```ts
export type SandboxAction =
  | { type: 'session'; sessionId: string | null }
  | { type: 'begin'; sessionId: string }
  | { type: 'event'; event: RunEvent }
  | { type: 'sse'; name: string; data: unknown }
  | { type: 'focus'; anchor: Anchor | null }
  | { type: 'reset' };
```
`applyEvent(state, event)` returns new state. `sandboxReducer(state, action)`.
'sse' → if name==='result' → phase done + summary; if 'fatal' → phase failed + error message; else wrap payload: data is already the event object with type (camel), and name === type. So `const event = data as RunEvent` — but if data lacks type, use name. Defensive:
```ts
function asRunEvent(name: string, data: unknown): RunEvent | null {
  if (typeof data !== 'object' || data === null) return null;
  const obj = data as Record<string, unknown>;
  const type = typeof obj['type'] === 'string' ? (obj['type'] as string) : name;
  return { ...obj, type } as unknown as RunEvent;
}
```

applyEvent cases:
- stage: append StageMark, activeStage = stage, activeRound = round.
- claims: claims = event.claims.map(toClaimView); rejectedClaims = event.rejected.
- probes: for each probe → find claim view; append probe view (dedupe by probe.id, since SSE may repeat? Not repeated but replay-safe); collect interviewers set; rejectedProbes appended.
- answers: for each answer outcome → find probe by probeId → set answer; errors → set answerError.
- budget: set budget; mark closed claims.
- verdicts: attach to claims; errors → push error message (non-fatal).
- suggestions: attach; errors → push error.
- error: push errors with fatal flag; if fatal → phase 'failed'.

Also `usage` — events don't carry usage except summary. So keep usage from summary; the App overlays telemetry from /api/stats. Let me add a separate field `telemetry: LlmStats | null` updated by an action {type:'telemetry', stats}. Cleaner.

- `claimById(state, id)`.
- `progress(state)`: {claims: total, probed, answered, judged, suggested} for the status bar.
- `countsByVerdict(state)`.
- `panelsInUse(state)`.

Then probes list: each probe → 面试官 name chip + round + question + rationale (灰) + anchor quote + answer block (要么 回答文本 + citedAnchors quotes, 要么 答不上来 badge, 要么 error). **App.tsx**
State: health, demo, sessions, input {resume, jd, jobTitle}, panel, pace, sandbox (useReducer), busy, currentSessionId, error banner.
Effects:
- on mount: load health + demo + sessions list.
- run(): createSession({resumeText, jdText, jobTitle}) → dispatch begin → streamRun(id, {panel}, onEvent → pump) ; pump: if pace>0 queue, else dispatch immediately. Simpler implementation: always push into a ref queue and drain with a timer whose interval = pace; if pace===0 drain synchronously on push. Let me implement:

```ts
const queueRef = useRef<SseEvent[]>([]);
const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
const streamingRef = useRef(false);

const drain = useCallback(() => {
  const ev = queueRef.current.shift();
  if (ev === undefined) { if (!streamingRef.current) stopTimer(); return; }
  dispatch({type:'sse', name: ev.event, data: ev.data});
  if (paceRef.current === 0) drain();  // 立即模式：一次排空
}, []);
```
Careful about recursion depth with pace=0 (events ~30, fine).

Push:
```ts
const pushEvent = (ev) => { queueRef.current.push(ev); if (timerRef.current === null) { if (paceRef.current === 0) drain(); else timerRef.current = window.setInterval(drain, paceRef.current); } };
```
When pace===0 and drain runs while streaming, after queue empties it returns (timerRef stays null) ✓. With interval started, after queue empties and streaming done → clearInterval.

Telemetry polling: setInterval 700ms → fetchStats(sessionId) → dispatch telemetry. Clear when phase done/failed.

Also handle 'result' event: the stream ends after result; on streamRun resolve, set streamingRef=false and drain remaining.

History load: onLoad(id) → getSession(id) → setSandbox(stateFromHistory(id, view.events, view.notices)) + set resume text from view.session.resumeText + jobTitle. Also set the intake fields so the resume pane shows.

Design: keep `resumeText` for the resume pane = sandbox from history uses view.session.resumeText; for a live run the intake's resume text. Let me have a state `material: {resumeText, jdText, jobTitle}` that is set on run and on history load. IntakePanel keeps its own form state, and calls onRun with values; App stores material for display.

highlight.ts:
```ts
import type { Anchor } from './api.ts';

export interface Segment { text: string; kind: 'plain' | 'hit' | 'focus' }
export interface Range { start: number; end: number }

export function clampRange(range: Range, length: number): Range | null {
  const start = Math.max(0, Math.min(range.start, length));
  const end = Math.max(0, Math.min(range.end, length));
  return end > start ? { start, end } : null;
}

export function mergeRanges(ranges: Range[]): Range[] {
  const sorted = [...ranges].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end);
  const out: Range[] = [];
  for (const r of sorted) {
    const last = out[out.length - 1];
    if (last !== undefined && r.start <= last.end) { if (r.end > last.end) last.end = r.end; continue; }
    out.push({...r});
  }
  return out;
}

/** 把简历原文切成普通/命中/聚焦三种片段；聚焦段覆盖命中段 */
export function buildSegments(text, focus: Range|null, hits: Range[]): Segment[]
```
Implementation: compute focusRange = focus clamped; hitRanges = merge(hits clamped, excluding those inside focus? overlapping is fine since focus takes precedence). Build boundary set from all ranges + 0 + len; then for each interval determine kind by precedence focus > hit > plain. Simple and robust:

```ts
const points = new Set<number>([0, text.length]);
for (const r of [...hitList, ...(focusList?[focusList]:[])]) { points.add(r.start); points.add(r.end); }
const cuts = [...points].filter(p => p >= 0 && p <= text.length).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
const segments: Segment[] = [];
for (let i = 0; i < cuts.length - 1; i++) {
  const a = cuts[i]!; const b = cuts[i+1]!;
  if (b <= a) continue;
  const kind = focusList && focusList.start <= a && b <= focusList.end ? 'focus' : hitList.some(r => r.start <= a && b <= r.end) ? 'hit' : 'plain';
  ...merge consecutive same-kind
}
```
Note `cuts[i]!` — non-null assertion is erasable? `!` is a type assertion operator, erasable ✓ (allowed with erasableSyntaxOnly).
Actually with noUncheckedIndexedAccess, need `!` or checks.

Then ResumePane renders segments: focus → <mark className="anchor-hit">, hit → <mark className="anchor-soft">, plain → text.

Auto-scroll: use a ref on an element only when focus changes; simplest: render a marker span with ref={focusRef} at the focus segment and useEffect on focus → scrollIntoView. Since ResumePane is inside a scrollable pane, and the pane may not be the scroll container... Put the scroll container in ResumePane with overflow-auto and call scrollIntoView on the marked element with block:'center'. React sets refs; I need the ref to update on re-render. Use a callback ref keyed by focus change: `ref={(el) => { if (el !== null && focus !== null) el.scrollIntoView({block:'center'}) }}` — this runs on every render of that element; may cause repeated scrolling. Better useEffect approach with a state-scoped ref: keep `const focusRef = useRef<HTMLElement|null>(null)` assigned via callback ref on the focus mark, and useEffect(() => { focusRef.current?.scrollIntoView({block:'center', behavior:'smooth'}) }, [focus?.start, focus?.end]).

Careful about `behavior: 'smooth'` + prefers-reduced-motion — fine.

App.tsx: wiring as designed.

Key implementation details for App:

```tsx
const [health, setHealth] = useState<HealthInfo | null>(null);
const [demo, setDemo] = useState<DemoInfo | null>(null);
const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<SessionSummary[]>([]);
const [draft, setDraft] = useState<IntakeDraft>({ resumeText: '', jdText: '', jobTitle: '' });
const [panel, setPanel] = useState<InterviewerRole[]>(['tech', 'hr', 'hiring_manager']);
const [pace, setPace] = useState(0);
const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
const [hint, setHint] = useState<string | null>(null);
const [material, setMaterial] = useState<{ resumeText: string; title: string }>({ resumeText: '', title: '' });
const [sandbox, dispatch] = useReducer(sandboxReducer, initialSandbox);
const queueRef = useRef<SseEvent[]>([]);
const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
const streamingRef = useRef(false);
const paceRef = useRef(pace);
paceRef.current = pace;   // hmm, assignment during render is OK-ish; better useEffect
```
Use `useEffect(() => { paceRef.current = pace }, [pace])`.

Drain function via useCallback with [dispatch]:
```tsx
const stopTimer = useCallback(() => { if (timerRef.current !== null) { window.clearInterval(timerRef.current); timerRef.current = null; } }, []);
const drain = useCallback(() => {
  const next = queueRef.current.shift();
  if (next === undefined) { if (!streamingRef.current) stopTimer(); return; }
  dispatch({ type: 'sse', name: next.event, data: next.data });
  if (paceRef.current === 0) drain();
}, [dispatch, stopTimer]);
```
Note: recursive drain with pace 0 — recursion for ~40 events is fine.

pushEvent:
```tsx
const pushEvent = useCallback((event: SseEvent) => {
  queueRef.current.push(event);
  if (paceRef.current === 0) { drain(); return; }
  if (timerRef.current === null) timerRef.current = window.setInterval(drain, paceRef.current);
}, [drain]);
```

run:
```tsx
const handleRun = async () => {
  if (busy) return;
  setBusy(true); setHint(null);
  try {
    const created = await createSession({ resumeText: draft.resumeText, jdText: draft.jdText, jobTitle: draft.jobTitle });
    setMaterial({ resumeText: draft.resumeText, title: created.jobTitle });
    queueRef.current = [];
    streamingRef.current = true;
    dispatch({ type: 'begin', sessionId: created.id });
    void refreshSessions();
    pollRef.current = window.setInterval(() => { void fetchStats(created.id).then((s) => dispatch({ type: 'telemetry', stats: s })).catch(() => undefined) }, 700);
    await streamRun(created.id, { panel }, pushEvent);
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : '质询没能开始';
    dispatch({ type: 'sse', name: 'fatal', data: { message } });
  } finally {
    streamingRef.current = false;
    drain();  // 把队列里剩下的放完
    window.clearInterval(pollRef.current...)
    setBusy(false);
    void refreshSessions();
  }
};
```
Hmm: `drain()` after streaming done only drains one event in pace mode; the interval keeps running and will finish the queue, then stop (since streamingRef false). But if pace>0 and timerRef is null (queue emptied earlier?) — edge: queue drained fully before stream ended, timer cleared? stopTimer only when queue empty AND !streaming. During streaming, when queue empties with pace>0, timer stays but does nothing; that's fine.

Edge: pace>0, queue empty, timer running — drain returns without stopping (streaming true). OK.

For finally: if pace===0, call drain() to flush; if pace>0 the interval handles it.

Also need to handle 'result' → phase done; then clear polling.

Telemetry clear: in the finally block clear interval.

```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ApiError, createSession, deleteSession, fetchDemo, fetchHealth, fetchStats, getSession, listSessions, reportUrl, streamRun } from './api.ts';
import type { DemoInfo, HealthInfo, InterviewerRole, SessionSummary, SseEvent } from './api.ts';
import { initialSandbox, sandboxReducer, stateFromHistory, totalProbes } from './state.ts';
import { ClaimBoard } from './components/ClaimBoard.tsx';
import { IntakePanel } from './components/IntakePanel.tsx';
import type { IntakeDraft } from './components/IntakePanel.tsx';
import { ResumePane } from './components/ResumePane.tsx';
import { StageRail } from './components/StageRail.tsx';
import { Chip } from './components/Bits.tsx';

const EMPTY_DRAFT: IntakeDraft = { resumeText: '', jdText: '', jobTitle: '' };
const DEFAULT_PANEL: InterviewerRole[] = ['tech', 'hr', 'hiring_manager'];

function modeLabel(health: HealthInfo | null): { text: string; tone: string } {
  if (health === null) return { text: '连接中…', tone: '' };
  if (health.mode === 'replay') return { text: '离线回放（不联网、不需要 Key）', tone: 'border-mint-500/40 text-mint-400' };
  if (health.mode === 'record') return { text: '联网录制中', tone: 'border-amber-ink/40 text-amber-ink' };
  return { text: '联网直连', tone: 'border-ember-500/40 text-ember-300' };
}

function numberText(value: number): string { return value.toLocaleString('zh-CN'); }
```

```tsx
export function App() {
  const [health, setHealth] = useState<HealthInfo | null>(null);
  const [demo, setDemo] = useState<DemoInfo | null>(null);
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<SessionSummary[]>([]);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<IntakeDraft>(EMPTY_DRAFT);
  const [panel, setPanel] = useState<InterviewerRole[]>(DEFAULT_PANEL);
  const [pace, setPace] = useState(0);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [hint, setHint] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [material, setMaterial] = useState<{ resumeText: string; title: string }>({ resumeText: '', title: '' });
  const [sandbox, dispatch] = useReducer(sandboxReducer, initialSandbox);

  const queueRef = useRef<SseEvent[]>([]);
  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const pollRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const streamingRef = useRef(false);
  const paceRef = useRef(pace);

  useEffect(() => { paceRef.current = pace; }, [pace]);

  const refreshSessions = useCallback(async () => {
    try { setSessions(await listSessions(20)); } catch { /* 列表拉不到不影响主流程 */ }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void (async () => {
      try {
        const [h, d] = await Promise.all([fetchHealth(), fetchDemo()]);
        setHealth(h); setDemo(d);
      } catch (error) { setHint(error instanceof Error ? error.message : '连不上本地服务'); }
      await refreshSessions();
    })();
  }, [refreshSessions]);

  useEffect(() => () => { if (timerRef.current !== null) window.clearInterval(timerRef.current); if (pollRef.current !== null) window.clearInterval(pollRef.current); }, []);

  const stopTimer = useCallback(...);
  const drain = useCallback(...);
  const pushEvent = useCallback(...);
  ...
}
```

handleRun:
```tsx
const handleRun = useCallback(async () => {
  if (busy) return;
  setBusy(true);
  setHint(null);
  let sessionId: string | null = null;
  try {
    const created = await createSession({
      resumeText: draft.resumeText,
      jdText: draft.jdText,
      jobTitle: draft.jobTitle.trim().length > 0 ? draft.jobTitle.trim() : undefined,
    });
    sessionId = created.id;
    setMaterial({ resumeText: draft.resumeText, title: created.jobTitle });
    queueRef.current = [];
    streamingRef.current = true;
    dispatch({ type: 'begin', sessionId: created.id });
    void refreshSessions();
    const id = created.id;
    pollRef.current = window.setInterval(() => {
      void fetchStats(id).then((stats) => dispatch({ type: 'telemetry', stats })).catch(() => undefined);
    }, 800);
    await streamRun(id, { panel }, pushEvent);
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : error instanceof Error ? error.message : '质询没能开始';
    dispatch({ type: 'sse', name: 'fatal', data: { message } });
  } finally {
    streamingRef.current = false;
    if (paceRef.current === 0) drain();
    if (pollRef.current !== null) { window.clearInterval(pollRef.current); pollRef.current = null; }
    setBusy(false);
    void refreshSessions();
    if (sessionId !== null) { void fetchStats(sessionId).then((stats) => dispatch({ type: 'telemetry', stats })).catch(() => undefined); }
  }
}, [busy, draft, panel, pushEvent, drain, refreshSessions]);
```

Order: static files then api routes? Hono middleware order: app.use('/*', serveStatic({root: dist})) then api routes, plus SPA fallback via serveStatic({root: dist, rewriteRequestPath: (p) => p}) hmm. Standard approach:

```ts
if (webDist !== null) {
  app.use('/*', serveStatic({ root: webDist }));
  app.get('*', serveStatic({ path: join(webDist, 'index.html') }));  // SPA 回退
}
```
But app is created by createApp() with api routes registered; adding a catch-all GET after would conflict with notFound handler (Hono's notFound is separate, so a catch-all route works). However createApp registers `/api/*` routes; the catch-all '*' GET would also match /api/unknown → but Hono matches more specific routes first; /api/unknown has no route → my catch-all '*' would serve index.html instead of 404 JSON. Acceptable? Better: register static only for non-/api paths: `app.use('/*', async (c, next) => { if (c.req.path.startsWith('/api')) return next(); return serveStatic(...)(c, next) })`. Simpler: put the static middleware inside createApp with a guard.

Cleanest: in serve.ts, create the app, then:
```ts
if (existsSync(indexHtml)) {
  app.use('*', serveStatic({ root: webRoot }));
  app.get('*', (c, next) => c.req.path.startsWith('/api/') ? next() : serveStatic({ path: indexHtml })(c, next));
}
```
Hmm; let me implement a small helper in http/static.ts: `mountWebApp(app, distDir)` that:
```ts
export function mountWebApp(app: Hono, distDir: string): boolean {
  const indexHtml = join(distDir, 'index.html');
  if (!existsSync(indexHtml)) return false;
  const files = serveStatic({ root: distDir });
  const spa = serveStatic({ path: indexHtml });
  app.use('*', async (c, next) => {
    if (c.req.path.startsWith('/api/')) return next();
    return files(c, next);
  });
  app.get('*', async (c, next) => {
    if (c.req.path.startsWith('/api/')) return next();
    return spa(c, next);
  });
  return true;
}
```
Careful: `serveStatic({path})` — the option name for a specific file is `path`. In @hono/node-server/serve-static, options: `{ root, path, rewriteRequestPath, ... }`. `path` is relative to root. If I pass an absolute path with no root... The docs: `serveStatic({ path: './static/index.html' })` relative to cwd. Absolute should work (join(root, path) semantics: it computes `getFilePath` which if `path` provided uses it as-is joined with root?). Let me instead use `rewriteRequestPath: () => '/index.html'` with root = distDir. That's the documented SPA pattern:
```ts
app.get('*', serveStatic({ root: distDir, rewriteRequestPath: () => '/index.html' }));
```
But careful: for /api/unknown we want JSON 404 → guard with next() for /api.

Also careful: `app.use('*', ...)` + `app.get('*', ...)` ordering: Hono runs middleware in registration order; api routes registered before these (createApp) so they match first ✓. Since I mount after creating app, /api routes are already registered → they win ✓ (Hono matches in order of registration for same-method routes).

Content of static.ts:

```ts
/**
 * 把已构建的前端挂到同一个端口上。
 *
 * 比赛演示要的是「一条命令打开整个产品」：评委不必同时开两个终端、也不必知道
 * 5173 与 8787 的区别。所以 npm run preview 会先 vite build，再由这个模块把
 * packages/web/dist 托管到服务端端口；/api 前缀一律放行给接口，不参与静态匹配。
 */
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { serveStatic } from '@hono/node-server/serve-static';
import type { Hono } from 'hono';

export function mountWebApp(app: Hono, distDir: string): boolean {
  const entry = join(distDir, 'index.html');
  if (!existsSync(entry)) return false;

  const isApi = (path: string): boolean => path.startsWith('/api') ;

  // 静态资源：命中就返回，没命中交给后面（含 SPA 回退）
  app.use('*', async (c, next) => {
    if (isApi(c.req.path)) return next();
    return serveStatic({ root: distDir })(c, next);
  });
  // 前端路由回退：任何非 /api 的 GET 都回 index.html
  app.get('*', async (c, next) => {
    if (isApi(c.req.path)) return next();
    return serveStatic({ root: distDir, rewriteRequestPath: () => '/index.html' })(c, next);
  });
  return true;
}
```

Hmm: `serveStatic({root})` requires absolute root? It resolves root relative to cwd if relative. We'll pass absolute.

Note: serveStatic returns middleware; calling it inline each request creates a new middleware closure each time — slightly wasteful but fine. Better to create once outside. Let me create `const files = serveStatic(...)` and `const spa = serveStatic(...)` once.

Also careful: types — `serveStatic` options type: `ServeStaticOptions`. `root` is string. OK.

Potential issue: app.use('*') in Hono matches all paths and methods. Fine.

But there's an ordering concern: createApp registers /api routes BEFORE mountWebApp is called (in serve.ts we call createApp then mountWebApp) → Hono matches registered routes in order, so /api/health hits the API route first ✓. For /api/unknown: no route, middleware `app.use('*')` runs and calls next() (since isApi) → then app.get('*') also next() → falls to notFound handler ✓ which returns the Chinese JSON 404.

Then bin/serve.ts edit: add import + mount call + banner line. Use edit on the file.

Let me write the script carefully. Key CDP methods:
- Page.enable, Runtime.enable, Log.enable
- Page.navigate {url}
- Runtime.evaluate {expression, awaitPromise:true, returnByValue:true}
- Page.captureScreenshot {format:'png', captureBeyondViewport:true} → base64
- Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride {width:1600,height:1000,deviceScaleFactor:1,mobile:false} to control viewport.

Flow with waits: implement a helper send(method, params) with id matching.

Script outline:

```js
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';

const EDGE = 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Microsoft\\Edge\\Application\\msedge.exe';
const PORT = 9222;
const URL_TARGET = process.argv[2] ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8787/';
const OUT = process.argv[3] ?? '.tmp/ui';
mkdirSync(OUT, {recursive:true});
const profile = '.tmp/edge-profile';
const child = spawn(EDGE, ['--headless=new','--disable-gpu','--no-first-run','--no-default-browser-check','--remote-debugging-port='+PORT,'--user-data-dir='+profile,'about:blank'], {stdio:'ignore'});
...
async function waitVersion(){ for (let i=0;i<60;i++){ try { const r = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:'+PORT+'/json/version'); if (r.ok) return await r.json(); } catch {} await sleep(300);} throw new Error('edge 没起来'); }
const list = await (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:'+PORT+'/json/list')).json();
const page = list.find(t=>t.type==='page');
const ws = new WebSocket(page.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
await new Promise(res => ws.addEventListener('open', res));
let id=0; const pending=new Map(); const events=[];
ws.addEventListener('message', ev => { const msg = JSON.parse(ev.data); if (msg.id && pending.has(msg.id)) { const {resolve,reject}=pending.get(msg.id); pending.delete(msg.id); msg.error? reject(new Error(JSON.stringify(msg.error))): resolve(msg.result); } else if (msg.method) events.push(msg); });
const send = (method, params={}) => new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ const myId=++id; pending.set(myId,{resolve,reject}); ws.send(JSON.stringify({id:myId,method,params})); });
```
Then viewport, enable, navigate, wait for a predicate on document.body.innerText.

Helper:
```js
async function evalJs(expr){ const r = await send('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:expr, awaitPromise:true, returnByValue:true}); if (r.exceptionDetails) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(r.exceptionDetails)); return r.result.value; }
async function text(){ return await evalJs('document.body.innerText'); }
async function waitFor(pred, label, timeoutMs=60000){ const t0=Date.now(); for(;;){ let t=''; try{ t=await text(); }catch{} if (pred(t)) return t; if (Date.now()-t0>timeoutMs) throw new Error('等不到 '+label+' 超时；当前文本：' + t.slice(0,400)); await sleep(400);} }
async function shot(name){ const r = await send('Page.captureScreenshot',{format:'png'}); writeFileSync(OUT+'/'+name, Buffer.from(r.data,'base64')); }
async function clickByText(label){ const ok = await evalJs('(() => { const bs = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("button")); const b = bs.find(x => (x.textContent||"").trim() === ' + JSON.stringify(label) + '); if (!b) return "NOTFOUND:" + bs.map(x=>x.textContent.trim()).join(" / "); b.click(); return "clicked"; })()'); console.log('click ' + label + ' -> ' + ok); }
```
Careful: `String.raw` in the file content — the script has backticks if I write it in run_code... I'll build it with an array of lines joined by NL instead (no template literals in the target file content, avoiding backticks). Need to be careful with quotes inside expressions. Use string concatenation inside the generated JS.

Then flow:
- await send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', {width:1600,height:1000,deviceScaleFactor:1,mobile:false});
- Page.enable, Runtime.enable, Log.enable
- Page.navigate {url}
- waitFor(t => t.includes('开始质询'))
- shot('01-idle.png')
- clickByText('一键示例'); sleep 300; shot('02-demo-loaded.png')
- clickByText('开始质询'); sleep 1500; shot('03-running.png')
- waitFor(t => t.includes('已完成') || t.includes('下载报告'), 'run finished', 90000)
- sleep 1500; shot('04-done.png')
- scroll to claims: evalJs window.scrollTo / and screenshot the middle column: shot('05-claims.png') after scrolling down.
- print console errors: filter events for Runtime.consoleAPICalled with type 'error' and Runtime.exceptionThrown and Log.entryAdded level error.
- kill child, print summary.

Then read_image the screenshots.

One risk: headless Edge with `--headless=new`. Fine.

Another: the DB data/crossexam.db now has sessions from earlier smokes... not a problem.

```text
29|   for (const range of sorted) {
30|     const last = merged[merged.length - 1];

32|       if (range.end > last.end) last.end = range.end;
33|       continue;
34|     }
35|     merged.push({ start: range.start, end: range.end });
36|   }
37|   return merged;
38| }
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL, VERDICT_LABEL } from '@crossexam/shared/domain.ts';
import { PERSONA_PRESETS } from '@crossexam/shared/contract.ts';

describe('界面文案与领域层不漂移', () => {
  it('判定与类别的中文标签就是领域层那一份（不是抄的）', () => {
    expect(labels.VERDICT_LABEL).toBe(VERDICT_LABEL);
    expect(labels.CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL).toBe(CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL);
  });
  it('五类人设的名字与一句话简介与 PERSONA_PRESETS 一致', () => {
    for (const [role, persona] of Object.entries(PERSONA_PRESETS)) {
      expect(labels.PERSONA_LABEL[role as keyof typeof labels.PERSONA_LABEL]).toBe(persona.name);
      expect(labels.PERSONA_BLURB[...]).toBe(persona.blurb);
    }
  });
  it('每个判定都有配色与一句话解释', ...);
  it('每个类别都有中文标签，且排序清单不漏不重', ...);
  it('五个阶段都有中文名与提示语', ...);
  it('会话状态都有中文名', ...);
});
```

Cases:
- clampRange: out of range clips; end<=start → null; negative start clamps to 0.
- mergeRanges: overlapping merged, adjacent merged (range.start <= last.end → adjacent merges since start === last.end → merges), non-overlapping kept in order, input order irrelevant.
- buildSegments: text '' → []; no hits → one plain segment equal to text; a hit in the middle → plain/hit/plain and concatenation equals text; focus inside a hit → focus wins; focus partially overlapping a hit → the overlapping part is hit, the focus-only part is focus (verify boundaries); consecutive same-kind merged.
- big test: joining all segment texts always reconstructs the original exactly (property-ish with a few ranges).
- describeRange: 0-based start → '第 1–5 字'.

Cases:
- begin → running, sessionId set, claims empty.
- claims event → claims mapped, anchor stripped (identity? stripAnchor returns a new object) — check deep equal.
- probes event with 2 probes for C1/C2 → interviewers learned in order, dedup on repeat interviewer.
- probes for unknown claim id ignored.
- duplicate probe id ignored.
- empty probes → notice appended 「第 N 轮没有任何新的追问，质询到此结束」.
- answers event → answer attached to matching probe, answerError cleared.
- answers errors → answerError set.
- budget event → closed flags + budget stored.
- verdicts event with error → errors list non-fatal message.
- suggestions event with null suggestion → suggestion stored with null.
- error fatal → phase failed.
- sse 'result' → phase done, summary/usage/notices replaced (authoritative notices override inferred).
- sse 'fatal' → phase failed + error.
- stateFromHistory: rows of events ending with 'result'? stateFromHistory doesn't handle result rows (rows are events only; the result is emitted as SSE only, not persisted). So history replay → phase 'done' by default, notices passed as argument (authoritative). Test: replay of [stage, claims, probes, budget, verdicts, suggestions] yields done + progress correct + notices from arg.

- progressOf: claims/probed/answered/judged/suggestions counts.
- verdictCounts ordering: only present kinds, in fixed order.
- totalProbes, allAnchors count = claims + probes.
- asRunEvent: null for non-object; falls back to name; runEventFromRow uses kind.

```ts
function claim(id: string, quote: string, start = 0): Claim {
  return { id, text: '主张 ' + id, anchor: { start, end: start + quote.length, quote, exact: true }, category: 'metric', weight: 0.9 };
}
function probe(id: string, claimId: string, interviewerId: string, round = 1, parentProbeId: string | null = null): Probe {
  return { id, claimId, interviewerId, round, question: '问题 ' + id, anchor: { start: 0, end: 4, quote: '原文引用', exact: true }, parentProbeId, rationale: '因为 ' + id };
}
function answerFor(p: Probe): AnswerOutcome { return { answer: { probeId: p.id, text: '回答 ' + p.id, mode: 'simulated', citedAnchors: [] }, answered: true, droppedCitations: [] }; }
function verdictFor(claimId: string, kind: VerdictKind): VerdictOutcome { return { verdict: { claimId, kind, confidence: 0.8, rationale: '理由', evidence: [], probeIds: [] }, adjusted: false, adjustmentReason: null, droppedEvidence: [] }; }
function suggestFor(claimId: string): SuggestOutcome { return { claimId, status: 'suggested', suggestion: { claimId, anchor: {...}, problem: 'p', before: 'b', after: 'a', why: 'w' }, reason: null }; }
```

```ts
const A = (quote: string, start = 0, end = quote.length): Anchor => ({ start, end, quote, exact: true });

function claim(id: string, category: ClaimCategory = 'metric'): Claim {
  return { id, text: '主张' + id, anchor: A('原文' + id), category, weight: 0.9 };
}
function probe(id: string, claimId: string, interviewerId: string, round = 1, parentProbeId: string | null = null): Probe {
  return { id, claimId, interviewerId, round, question: '问题' + id, anchor: A('引文' + id), parentProbeId, rationale: '因为' + id };
}
function answered(p: Probe, isAnswered = true): AnswerOutcome {
  return { answer: { probeId: p.id, text: '回答' + p.id, mode: 'simulated', citedAnchors: [] }, answered: isAnswered, droppedCitations: [] };
}
function verdictOf(claimId: string, kind: VerdictKind, adjusted = false): VerdictOutcome {
  return { verdict: { claimId, kind, confidence: 0.8, rationale: '理由' + claimId, evidence: [A('证据原文')], probeIds: ['P1'] }, adjusted, adjustmentReason: adjusted ? '模型拿不出可定位的证据，按证据缺失处理' : null, droppedEvidence: [] };
}
function suggestionOf(claimId: string): SuggestOutcome { ... }
```
Note: `A('原文' + id)` sets end = quote.length which is a length not matching actual needs — irrelevant for tests.

```ts
function reduce(events: RunEvent[]): SandboxState {

}
```

Commit messages (Conventional Commits, Chinese, ≤50 chars):

C1: chore(web): 建立前端包与构建配置
body: 前端独立 tsconfig（含 .tsx 与 vite/client 类型），根 typecheck 串起各包；新增 start 与 preview 脚本，让一个端口就能打开整个产品。

C7: chore(repo): 忽略本地运行数据与临时目录
body: 服务默认把 SQLite 落在 data/，里面是用户简历正文，属于隐私数据，不该进仓库；.tmp/ 放浏览器截图与诊断脚本。

C2: feat(web): 声明 HTTP 线上契约与 SSE 解析
body: 跑沙盘是 POST，浏览器的 EventSource 只能发 GET，所以用 fetch 读 ReadableStream 手切 SSE 块；类型刻意在 web 侧重声明，不把 shared 引进浏览器包（shared 经 util 依赖 node:crypto）。

C3: feat(web): 事件流折叠成界面状态
body: 直播（SSE）与翻历史（事件表）共用同一个纯函数折叠器，界面不会出现两套逻辑；锚点切分单独成模块，便于对着区间做单测。

C4: feat(web): 三栏质询界面，提问与判定都带原文锚点
body: 左栏投料与面试官面板，中栏主张卡片与追问链，右栏简历原文联动高亮；判定被代码收敛时界面显式说明，不把模型的原始说法当成最终结论。

C5: feat(server): 一个端口同时提供接口与已构建的前端
body: /api 前缀放行给接口，其余请求走静态资源并对前端路由回退到 index.html；评审因此不必开两个终端。

C6: test(web): 守住文案漂移与事件折叠
body: 中文标签必须与领域层同一份、人设文案必须与契约逐字一致（防抄写漂移）；区间切分保证不丢字；事件折叠覆盖预算关停、作答失败与致命错误。

Let me commit.

```text
=== ADR-0002 (52) ===
1| # ADR-0002 离线演示走脚本合成卡带，并用漂移门禁守住它
2| 
3| - 状态：已采纳
4| - 日期：2026-10-26
5| - 相关：ADR-0001、提交物 #3「README 10 分钟极速复现指南」、packages/server/src/llm/scripted-demo.ts
6| 
7| ## 背景
8| 
9| 评分里 25% 是「过程可复现性」，提交物要求一份 10 分钟极速复现指南。
10| 本项目的核心体验是多 Agent 对抗式追问——如果评委员没有 API Key，默认模式下点一次
11| 「示例简历」就该看到完整的一场质询，而不是一句「请配置环境变量」。
12| 
13| 同时我们有一条隐私红线：真实简历内容只落本地 SQLite，不上传第三方，也不进公开仓库。
14| 这两条约束合起来意味着：**演示素材必须是合成的，且不能依赖联网**。
15| 
16| ## 决策
17| 
18| 1. **默认模式是 replay**（CROSSEXAM_MODE 缺省即 replay），卡带放在仓库的 fixtures/llm/。
19| 2. **演示卡带由脚本合成**（packages/server/src/llm/scripted-demo.ts）：一份写死的剧本，
20|    按**真实提示词**渲染回答，再按提示词指纹（sha256 前 24 位）落盘。
21| 3. **合成数据必须一眼可辨**：卡带里的 model 字段固定为 scripted-demo，
22|    recordedAt 固定为一个常量（因此重跑逐字节相同），且 fixtures/README.md 明写这一点。
23| 4. **生成器自检**：录完立刻换只读回放客户端在另一个内存库里重跑一遍，
24|    比对两次结果签名（主张、追问链、判定、建议、通知、预算、用量）；
25|    另外逐阶段点名 extract/probe/answer/verdict/suggest 是否都录到，缺一即退出码 1。
26| 5. **漂移门禁**：packages/server/test/demo-cassette.test.ts 用**仓库里已提交的卡带**
27|    走真实 HTTP 跑一遍完整沙盘。改了提示词而没重跑 npm run seed:demo，这条测试就红。
28| 
29| ## 后果
30|
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now write the docs. I'll create ADR-0003 and append sections to progress.md, findings.md (F14, F15), and edit task_plan.md.

ADR-0003 content (Web 前端形态与单端口演示):
- 状态/日期/相关
- 背景：UI/UX 15% + 明文要求流式与思考可视化；评审要 10 分钟复现；评审可能只看一次
- 决策：
 1. 前端独立包 packages/web（Vite + React 19 + Tailwind v4），web 侧重新声明 HTTP 契约而不 import @crossexam/shared（shared 经 util.ts 依赖 node:crypto，进浏览器包即崩）；真正需要一致的中文标签与判定语义：标签直接子路径复用 shared/domain.ts（零依赖），人设文案抄写一份并由 packages/web/test/labels.test.ts 漂移测试看守。
 2. 直播与翻历史共用同一个纯函数折叠器（packages/web/src/state.ts），SSE 与事件表只是同一串事件的两种来源。
 3. 开跑是 POST → 不用 EventSource，用 fetch + ReadableStream 手写 SSE 解析。
 4. main.tsx 不包 StrictMode（有副作用的 POST 只能跑一次；双挂载第二次会 409）。
 5. 单端口演示：服务端托管 packages/web/dist（@hono/node-server/serve-static），/api 放行，其余回退 index.html；npm run preview = build + start。
- 后果：
 收益：界面与报告用同一套词；历史回看不需要第二套渲染逻辑；评审一条命令就能看到完整产品；web 侧类型与 shared 的漂移由 labels 测试 + server 的 203 条测试 + 端到端 demo 卡带门禁共同兜住。
 代价：web 侧类型是抄的，字段增删要靠人同步（有漂移测试但只覆盖标签与人设）；不引 shared 意味着不能在前端复用锚定逻辑（目前不需要）。
- 备选：把 shared 拆成 browser-safe/core 两部分（更干净但 P3 之前会拖延，留待后续）；EventSource + GET 跑沙盘（会引入 GET 触发副作用的坏设计）。
